class="flex justify-between gap-12"><div class="max-w-2xl min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-12 overflow-x-hidden"><article class="border-b border-gray-200"><h1 class="heading-2xl text-default mb-3 leading-tight font-extrabold">ChatGPT release notes</h1><p class="text-secondary mb-3">Review the latest updates and release notes for ChatGPT.</p><div class="text-tertiary text-sm tracking-wide mb-10">Updated: 5 hours ago</div><div class="prose prose-blue article-content text-prose mb-12 max-w-none text-base font-normal"><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>October 27, 2026</h1><h2>iCloud Mail and Calendar plugins in ChatGPT</h2><p>iCloud users can now connect Mail and Calendar to ChatGPT to find emails, catch up on your inbox, check your schedule, and update calendar events. </p><p>You can learn more about how ChatGPT uses connected apps <a href="/en/articles/11487775-connected-apps-in-chatgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>here</u></a>. Available to all ChatGPT plans on the web, desktop app, and iOS. </p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>October 26, 2026</h1><h2>GPT-6.1 Terra</h2><p>GPT-6.1 Terra is rolling out in ChatGPT Work and Codex. It supports a range of tasks, including visual reasoning, computer use, and writing.<br/><br/>The rollout includes Plus, Pro, Business, Enterprise, and Edu plans. As it reaches your account, you can use Terra in the Work and Codex experiences available with your plan, region, and workspace settings.<br/><br/>Select <b>GPT-6.1 Terra</b> from the model list. Your plan’s overall usage limits are unchanged. Note that some Plus and Pro users may see GPT-6.1 Terra at Medium instead of GPT-6.1 Sol at Light in the default Power presets in Work. Standard and Fast modes are supported where available.<br/><br/>Terra does not support UAE inference residency; this restriction is based on where inference runs, not your location.<br/><br/>For help using the model, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001275" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ChatGPT Work and Codex</a>. For current usage estimates, see: <a href="https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/pricing" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Pricing</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>October 21, 2026</h1><h2>MoMo payments in Vietnam</h2><p>You can now use MoMo to pay for ChatGPT Go, Plus, and Pro subscriptions purchased on chatgpt.com in Vietnam.<br/><br/>Subscriptions purchased through the iOS or Android app continue to be managed by the Apple App Store or Google Play. For supported currencies and payment methods, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/10421635" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Multi-currency billing</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>October 20, 2026</h1><h2>Introducing l<!-- -->ocked chats</h2><p>Locked chats let you keep selected conversations in your account while hiding them from regular chat history, pinned chats, and search. Open them with your 4-digit account PIN or supported device authentication. Your retention and model-improvement settings stay unchanged.<br/><br/>Locked chats are rolling out gradually to Free, Go, Plus, and Pro users on the web, in the iOS and Android apps, and in the <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001276" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">new ChatGPT desktop app</a> for macOS and Windows. To lock a chat, open a chat’s more options menu (•••) and select <b>Lock chat</b>.<br/><br/><a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about locked chats</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>October 16, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>Create and collaborate on slides in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>ChatGPT Slides lets you create presentations and keep working on them with your team in Space. Start from a conversation, files, or an outline, or share an example deck to guide the style and layout.</p><ul><li><p><b>Create and refine.</b> Edit text, images, shapes, and charts directly, or ask ChatGPT to make changes. Generate custom images or add your own.</p></li><li><p><b>Work together.</b> Invite teammates to edit the same deck in real time and leave shared comments.</p></li><li><p><b>Present or export.</b> Present directly from ChatGPT. On the web, export to PowerPoint, PDF, or Google Slides. In the desktop app, open your deck in PowerPoint or Keynote when those apps are installed.</p></li></ul><p>Slides is rolling out to eligible Pro, Business, and Enterprise users with Space access on the web and in the desktop app. To get started, ask ChatGPT in <b>Work</b> to create a presentation in Space.</p><h2><b>Updates to GPT-6 Pro model in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>GPT-6 Pro in the chat tab now offers:</p><ul><li><p>Faster responses</p></li><li><p>Improved use of plugins and tools to carry out tasks.</p></li><li><p>Multiple agents that can work on different parts of a task in parallel. You can follow their progress in the conversation and open completed tasks to see what each agent did.</p></li></ul><p>This update is rolling out to eligible Pro users in ChatGPT. Availability in managed workspaces depends on workspace settings and rollout eligibility.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>October 15, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>GPT-Live-1.1 and new voices in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>We’re rolling out GPT-Live-1.1 and GPT-Live-1.1 Quip, a mini version of the model, in ChatGPT Voice, with more natural accents across languages. Live also uses a wider range of short phrases to let you know when it’s searching.</p><p>New Live voices include options for French, German, Hindi, Japanese, Korean, and Mexican Spanish, plus Bubbie in English. The voices you see may vary by region and account.</p><p>To choose a voice, open <b>Settings &gt; Voice</b>, then select <b>Voice</b>.</p><p>For more information, see:<a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001274" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"> <u>ChatGPT Voice</u></a>.</p><h2>Applications for the Rosalind Academic Plan</h2><p>The Rosalind Academic Plan helps eligible academic labs use ChatGPT for life sciences research. Approved labs can receive up to 30 ChatGPT Business Standard seats at no cost for up to 12 months, with GPT-Rosalind access subject to trusted-access approval.</p><p>Applications are now open to labs at U.S. universities. If you’re a principal investigator or lab lead, <a href="https://chatgpt.com/rosalind/lab/application" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>apply on behalf of your lab</u></a>. Applying doesn’t give your lab immediate access. After approval, follow the instructions to set up your workspace and enable access for approved members. Labs covered by an OpenAI Enterprise agreement should use their university’s access process. For eligibility and setup details, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001602" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Rosalind Academic Plan</u></a>.</p><h2>Site limits (Plus and Pro)</h2><p>There is no longer a fixed limit on the number of Sites you can create. Usage and storage limits continue to apply.<br/><br/>For usage and limits, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Creating and using ChatGPT Sites</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>October 9, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>Composer predictions in Codex</b></h2><p>Composer predictions, available in beta, suggests your next message based on the conversation in your current Codex thread. After Codex responds, a suggestion may appear in the message box. Press Tab to accept it, then review or edit it before sending. Accepting a suggestion doesn’t send it automatically.</p><p>The beta is available to personal ChatGPT Pro users aged 18 and older in all supported regions, in the latest Codex desktop app. It supports local and SSH threads using GPT-6 Astra or GPT-6.1 Sol. Predictions are on by default; turn off <b>Show predictions</b> in the <b>Composer</b> section of <b>General</b> settings. During the beta, generating predictions doesn’t count toward Codex usage limits or consume credits. Messages you send, including accepted predictions, follow normal Codex usage limits and billing.</p><p>Read more: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001601" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Composer predictions in Codex</a></p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>October 7, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>GPT-6 and Intelligent UI in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>We’re introducing <a href="https://openai.com/index/gpt-6-for-everyone/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">GPT-6 with Intelligent UI in ChatGPT</a>. ChatGPT can now combine text, visuals, and interactive elements to fit your question, from side-by-side comparisons and diagrams to tools you can use directly in the conversation, such as calculators, bill splitters, and games. It can choose the format automatically, and still use a simple text answer when that works best.<br/><br/>GPT-6 can also begin answering while it continues to think or use tools, then add more findings without another prompt. You can start reading before the full answer is ready.<br/><br/>The rollout starts globally today for Plus, Pro, Business, and Enterprise in the Chat tab, and expands to Free and Go starting tomorrow. Enterprise availability depends on workspace admin settings.<br/><br/>Plus, Pro, Business, and Enterprise use GPT-6 Sol; Free and Go use GPT-6 Luna. Intelligent UI is available from Instant through Extra High reasoning. The Pro reasoning option continues to use GPT-6 Astra and doesn’t support Intelligent UI. The models powering Work and Codex aren’t changing with this release.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>October 6, 2026</h1><h2>Audio uploads in ChatGPT</h2><p>Upload audio files to ChatGPT to create transcripts, summarize recordings, and ask questions about their contents. You can turn a meeting, interview, or lecture into structured notes or a follow-up email draft. To get started, attach a supported audio file to a conversation and describe what you need.<br/><br/>Audio uploads are available with paid ChatGPT subscriptions and workspaces. Availability may vary by workspace settings, region, client version, and model. Transcripts may contain errors, and performance may vary across languages.<br/><br/>For more information, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/8555545" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Uploading files and audio to ChatGPT</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>October 2, 2026</h1><h2><b>Finances expands to Free and Go users</b></h2><p>Finances in ChatGPT is rolling out to Free and Go users in the U.S. on web, iOS, and Android. You can securely connect your financial accounts to find ways to save, understand your spending, and make sense of your investments, with answers grounded in your finances.</p><p><a href="/en/articles/20001222-finances-in-chatgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about Finances in ChatGPT.</a></p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>October 1, 2026</h1><h2><b>New ways to shop in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>See how clothes and accessories could look on you and save your favorites for later.</p><ul><li><p><b>Virtually try on clothing and accessories:</b> A “Try on” button will appear on product listings for clothes and accessories in ChatGPT. Select it, then take or upload a selfie to generate a virtual try-on with ChatGPT Images. Your reference photo is saved for future try-ons. You can change or delete it in Settings → Personalization → Reference photos.</p></li><li><p><b>Save your finds:</b> Save any product to Favorites or create a folder to organize your finds in your ChatGPT Library.</p></li></ul><p>Available in ChatGPT on mobile and web.</p><h2><b>Scan notes and documents more easily with ChatGPT camera</b></h2><p>It’s now easier to bring your notes and documents into ChatGPT on your phone. Capture multiple pages in a row, and ChatGPT automatically combines them into a single PDF, ready to upload to your chat. </p><p>To get started, open the camera in ChatGPT, tap the three-dot menu, and select Scan. Rolling out on iOS. </p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 29, 2026</h1><h2>A new Pro plan with Astra Ultrafast</h2><p>We’re introducing Pro 500, a new $500/month plan with the highest included usage of the Pro plans and access to Astra Ultrafast in ChatGPT Work and Codex. Select Ultrafast in the model picker to use a faster service tier for GPT-6 Astra. It uses your included allowance first, then your credit balance after that allowance is used.</p><p>Among Pro plans, Ultrafast is available only with Pro 500. Buying credits on Pro 100 or Pro 200 does not unlock it. Pro 500 subscriptions are available through ChatGPT on the web at launch.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/9793128" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ChatGPT Pro tiers</a>.</p><h2>Codex Cloud</h2><p>Codex Cloud lets you start and continue coding tasks from desktop, web, or mobile using reusable environments with your project’s repositories, tools, and dependencies. Each task gets its own isolated workspace, and tasks can continue while your computer is asleep. In managed workspaces, your workspace’s Codex cloud-access settings apply.</p><p>To connect repositories and prepare an environment, see: <a href="https://developers.openai.com/codex/environments/cloud-environments" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Set up and configure Codex Cloud</a>. For availability and usage, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/11369540" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Using Codex with your ChatGPT plan</a>.</p><h2>Create and collaborate on Pages</h2><p>Turn a conversation into a Page, start from a template, or begin writing directly in Space. Edit the text yourself or ask ChatGPT to revise it, add charts, or create interactive content. Collaborators can work on the same Page and leave comments, with each person using their own ChatGPT.</p><p>Pages follow Space’s availability and sharing settings. Sharing a Page does not give other people access to your private chats or memory; information added to the Page is visible to people who can view it.</p><h2>Creating, editing, and scheduling Sites</h2><p>Site owners and editors on Plus and Pro can select <b>Edit site</b> from a published Site in a desktop browser to continue editing in ChatGPT, review changes, and publish when ready.</p><p>Site owners can also create recurring cloud schedules from <b>Automations</b>. These updates are available where Sites is supported. Learn more about <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">creating and managing Sites</a>.</p><h2>GPT-6.1 Sol in ChatGPT Work and Codex</h2><p>GPT-6.1 Sol improves on GPT-6 Sol in agentic coding, computer use, and professional work. We’re rolling it out in ChatGPT Work and Codex, starting with Pro users and expanding to Plus, Business, Enterprise, and Edu. <!-- -->Enterprise and Edu workspace owners can enable model access in Workspace settings &gt; Permissions &amp; roles, on the Workspace tab or through a custom role. Learn how to <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/11750701" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">manage model access with role-based access control</a>.</p><p>For availability and model selection, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001275" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ChatGPT Work and Codex</a>.</p><h2>Meet your dot</h2><p>We’re introducing dots: always-on agents in ChatGPT that can take on ongoing work and keep making progress between conversations. Give your dot a goal, connect the apps it needs, and define what it can do on its own. Powered by GPT-6 Astra, your dot has its own cloud computer and can bring results back for your review.</p><p>Dots are rolling out gradually to eligible Pro and Business Premium users aged 18 and older in supported markets. Pro access excludes the European Economic Area, Switzerland, and the UK at launch. Enterprise access is a beta and is off by default. Create your dot in the ChatGPT desktop app or on desktop web.</p><p>For the next month, dots usage won’t count toward eligible Pro, Business, and Enterprise users’ plan allowances. After this period, we’ll share usage terms for each plan.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="/en/articles/20001530" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">getting started with your dot</a>.</p><h2>Meetings plugin</h2><p>We’re making it easier to bring meeting context into ChatGPT so you can act on next steps faster. The Meetings plugin takes notes for you based on what’s said in the meeting and saves personalized summaries with action items in ChatGPT Space. You can keep them private or share them with your team, then ask ChatGPT to update a project plan or draft a follow-up.</p><p>Audio is deleted once your notes are ready and can’t be accessed or replayed. Available in beta in the ChatGPT desktop app on macOS to Pro and Business users, with Enterprise coming soon. <a href="https://chatgpt.com/plugins/plugin_connector_1p_27af362d1a3c8191b38ba625f489d958?open_in_app" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more</a>.</p><h2>Organize your work in ChatGPT Space</h2><p>Space brings your Pages, files, and related work together in ChatGPT. Create a space for a project, add the material you need, and share it with other people so you can keep building on the same work. Space replaces Library for accounts with access; your existing Projects remain separate.</p><p>Available to eligible Pro, Business, and Enterprise users in the ChatGPT desktop app and on the web. Sharing in managed workspaces follows your organization’s settings; Enterprise sharing is an opt-in preview.</p><h2>Pro 200 subscriptions</h2><p>Pro 200 is available for new subscriptions again at $200/month.</p><p>Included usage allowances are changing. Users affected by these changes will receive an email with additional details. The monthly price remains $200.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/9793128" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Pro plans and existing subscriptions</a>.</p><h2>Review pull requests with Codex</h2><p>Use Code Review in Codex to read pull-request summaries and diffs, review findings, and discuss changes. Open a pull request, inspect the changes, and ask Codex to explain a section or help address a potential issue before sharing feedback.</p><p>Available through the Code Review plugin on supported desktop and web experiences. Access to a repository follows your connected account and workspace permissions.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/code-review" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Code Review</a>.</p><h2>Scan repositories with Codex Security Cloud</h2><p>Codex Security Cloud can scan GitHub repositories on demand or on a schedule, while ongoing checks review new commits. Codex investigates findings, removes duplicates, and prepares proposed fixes for review. Scans run in the cloud and can continue after you close your laptop.</p><p>Access requires the applicable workspace permissions, repository setup, and billing configuration. Existing Codex Security customers opt in before paid usage. This feature provides access to Daybreak Blue models only within Codex Security Cloud.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/security/setup" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">setting up Codex Security Cloud</a>.</p><h2>Share your profile and showcase your Sites</h2><p>Your ChatGPT profile now brings together your bio, activity, and Sites you choose to showcase. Personal profiles are private by default. You can make yours public and share its link with other people signed in to ChatGPT. Shared profiles do not show conversation titles or content.</p><p>Profile sharing and the Sites showcase are available on web and desktop. In Business workspaces, administrators control whether coworkers can view workspace profiles. Enterprise access is coming later.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001539" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shareable profiles</a>.</p><h2>Sign in to other products with ChatGPT</h2><p>You can use your ChatGPT account to sign in to participating products without creating another password. The product receives your name, email address, and profile picture if you have one. Signing in does not share your ChatGPT conversations or memory; you approve any additional access separately.</p><p>Available to signed-in ChatGPT users, subject to organization settings. Sign-in access is separate from permission to use your ChatGPT plan allowance.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Sign in with ChatGPT</a>.</p><h2>Start automations from connected-app events</h2><p>Supported plugins can use MCP events to start an automation when something changes in a connected app. Describe what you want ChatGPT to follow and what it should do with matching updates—for example, prepare a summary when new information arrives in a channel you have asked it to monitor.</p><p>The connected account must have access to the event and any information used by the task.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="/en/articles/11487775-connected-apps-in-chatgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connected apps in ChatGPT</a>.</p><h2>Use your ChatGPT allowance in participating apps</h2><p>Eligible Plus and Pro users can authorize participating apps to use their ChatGPT model allowance without creating an API key. Choose which apps can use your allowance and set an app-specific limit. Usage counts toward your existing plan allowance; connecting an app does not add more usage.</p><p>This is a limited preview with participating integrations. Using purchased credits requires a separate opt-in. A partner may still charge for its own product or services.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001542" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">using your ChatGPT allowance in other apps</a>.</p><h2>Work with interactive plugin extensions</h2><p>Plugins can now include interactive panels, sidebar views, and viewers or editors for supported file types. Work with an app alongside your conversation, select the information you want ChatGPT to use, or complete a form provided by the plugin.</p><p>Plugins still require their applicable account connections and workspace permissions.</p><p>Learn more about <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001256" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">plugins in ChatGPT and Codex</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 28, 2026</h1><h2>Explore your health data with personalized summaries</h2><p>You can now select a chart, metric, or record in the <b>Health</b> tab to get personalized explanations and insights based on your connected health information.</p><p><a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001036" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about Health in ChatGPT.</a></p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 25, 2026</h1><h2>Security history in ChatGPT</h2><p>We’re introducing security history, a new way to review recent security activity for your OpenAI account. You can see sign-ins, sign-outs, and changes to multi-factor authentication (MFA), passkeys, and other security settings. Events include the time, location, and device details. Some details may be approximate or unavailable.</p><p>To view your history in ChatGPT on the web, go to <b>Settings &gt; Security and login</b>, then select <b>Security history</b>. For help reviewing unfamiliar activity and securing your account, see: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/8304786" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Keeping your OpenAI account secure</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 23, 2026</h1><h2><b>Use plugins in Voice and get work done by speaking</b></h2><p>Live now supports plugins on web, iOS, and Android. You can use the plugins and connected apps available to your account during a Voice conversation and follow written responses in the chat.</p><p>Voice is also available in Work on web and mobile. Ask it to create documents, presentations, and spreadsheets, use connected apps, or work in a browser. When you end a Voice call in Work, an unfinished task can continue in text.</p><p>Free and Go users can use Voice in Chat with the plugins their plan supports. Voice in Work requires both Voice and Work access. Existing app connections, permissions, and usage limits apply.</p><p>Learn more: <a href="/en/articles/20001274-chatgpt-voice" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>ChatGPT Voice</u></a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 22, 2026</h1><h2><b>GPT-6 Sol and Luna in Work and Codex</b></h2><p>We’re introducing GPT-6 Sol and GPT-6 Luna in ChatGPT Work and Codex. These models are separate from the models available in Chat.</p><p>Codex desktop keeps the model you selected manually.</p><p>Available models and reasoning effort options depend on your plan and workspace settings.</p><p><a href="/en/articles/20001275" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Learn more about ChatGPT Work and Codex.</u></a></p><h2><b>Create flashcards in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>You can now create interactive flashcards in ChatGPT. Ask for flashcards on a topic you want to learn, or upload your notes and ask ChatGPT to turn them into flashcards.</p><p>Tap a card to flip it, then select ✔️ if you know the answer or ❌ to practice it again later. Shuffle your cards to practice in a different order.</p><p>Flashcards are automatically saved in your library. To practice again, open them there or ask ChatGPT to review them back.</p><p>Available on mobile and web for all ChatGPT plans.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 21, 2026</h1><h2><b>Credit scores in Finances</b></h2><p>You can now track your credit score in ChatGPT. Securely connect your Experian® credit report and VantageScore® 3.0 credit score to get personalized insights into what’s affecting your score and how it relates to your finances and goals.</p><p>Your score and report update monthly, and credit monitoring can alert you to important changes, such as a new inquiry, account, or address. Your Experian connection is separate from your bank accounts, so you can use the credit-score feature without connecting accounts through Plaid.</p><p>This feature is rolling out in Finances to Plus and Pro users in the U.S. on web, iOS, and Android. Some eligible users may not see it right away.</p><p><a href="/en/articles/20001222-finances-in-chatgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Learn more about Finances in ChatGPT.</u></a></p><h2>Privacy Center in ChatGPT</h2><p>Privacy Center is rolling out to signed-in ChatGPT Free, Go, Plus, and Pro users. It brings together information about chat privacy, memory, personalization, data use, connected apps, and account security, with links to the settings that manage them. Your privacy controls remain in ChatGPT Settings.</p><p>On the web, open your account menu, select Help, then select Privacy Center. On mobile, open Settings, then select Privacy Center on iOS or Privacy center on Android. Available options depend on your plan, region, and workspace settings.</p><p>Learn more: <a href="https://help.openai.com/articles/20001488" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Privacy Center in ChatGPT</u></a></p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 17, 2026</h1><h2>ChatGPT for Word</h2><p>ChatGPT is now available in Microsoft Word. You can draft from notes, summarize a document, revise selected text, and adjust headings and formatting from the ChatGPT sidebar. Word joins Excel and PowerPoint through the same Microsoft add-in.</p><p>ChatGPT for Word is available on all ChatGPT plans, including Free. Your plan’s token limits apply, and Word draws from the shared usage allowance for Codex and other premium features on plans that include it.</p><p>To get started, install the ChatGPT add-in from <a href="https://marketplace.microsoft.com/en-us/product/office/WA200010215" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Microsoft Marketplace</u></a>, open ChatGPT in Word, and sign in.</p><h2>Connect multiple accounts to plugins in ChatGPT</h2><p>You can now connect multiple accounts to plugins in ChatGPT, expanding support beyond Gmail, Google Calendar, and Google Contacts. Bring your personal and work accounts into the same conversation, so ChatGPT can help you find information and get things done across your personal and work accounts. Available on all ChatGPT plans across web, mobile, and desktop.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 14, 2026</h1><h2>Updated permissions for new Health connections</h2><p>For new Health plugin connections, ChatGPT will now default to each user’s global plugins permissions setting.</p><p>If you make no changes to your Plugins settings, the default permission setting is “<a href="/en/articles/11487775-apps-in-chatgpt#permission-options" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Allow low-risk actions</u></a>.” This means that once you’ve connected your information in Health, ChatGPT can use it when relevant without asking for permission each time, unless you are attempting a sensitive action, such as asking ChatGPT to email a training plan based on your data to a running partner. You can now choose to personalize responses in a temporary chat with memory, plugins, and custom instructions from your regular chat settings. You can also choose to save a temporary chat.</p><h3>How it works</h3><ul><li><p><b>Personalized</b>: When you create a temporary chat, you can choose to have personalized responses.  Personalized temporary chats can use your memories, custom instructions, and plugins to tailor responses. They do not create new memories and remain out of your chat history unless you save them. Personalization can be set only when you start a temporary chat and cannot be changed after the conversation begins.</p></li><li><p><b>Non-personalized:</b> By default, temporary chats are not personalized. Non-personalized temporary chats do not use memory, custom instructions, or plugins, and they do not create new memories.</p></li><li><p><b>Saving a temporary chat: </b>When a conversation becomes useful for the future, you can add it to your chat history. Saving converts it into a regular chat, so it then follows your account-level personalization settings and model-improvement preferences. For example, if you have memory enabled and save a temporary chat, this will become part of your chat history and can be referenced to personalized future responses. </p></li></ul></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>August 25, 2026</h1><h2>Scheduled tasks can respond to app updates and be shared</h2><p>Scheduled tasks in ChatGPT Work can use webhooks to respond when something changes in a supported app, helping you keep work moving without repeatedly checking for updates. You can also select a folder and ask ChatGPT to work across the files it contains. Your content stays connected to Drive, so it’s easy to return to the original. Where supported and authorized, ChatGPT can update the source file directly.</p><h3><b>Availability</b></h3><p>The initial experience includes My Drive and files and folders shared directly with you; Shared Drives aren’t included yet. Some Google Drive editing and collaboration features aren’t yet available in ChatGPT.</p><p>Rolling out to Plus, Pro, Enterprise, Edu, Healthcare and Business users on the web in both the Chat and Work toggles. Mobile support will follow.  </p><h2><b>Computer History for macOS</b></h2><p>Computer History is an optional feature in the ChatGPT macOS app that lets ChatGPT and Codex reference selected activity from apps and websites, helping users continue work without re-explaining every detail. It records interaction events—not screenshots, screen recordings, microphone input, or system audio—and private browsing is not included.<br/><br/>Computer History is off by default and available to Pro, Business, and Enterprise users. Business and Enterprise admins must grant access before members can opt in. Users can pause Computer History, choose included apps and sites, and inspect or delete timeline items. Note that Computer History is not currently available in the EEA, UK or Switzerland.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>August 10, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>Restaurant reservations in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>ChatGPT can now help you find available restaurant reservations with OpenTable, Resy, and Yelp.<br/><br/>Ask in the chat and include where and when you’d like to eat, your party size, and any preferences, such as cuisine, budget, dietary needs, or atmosphere. You’ll see available reservation times directly in the response. You can also ask follow-up questions to narrow your options or check a specific restaurant. When you find a time that works, select it to book it.<br/><br/>Rolling out across all ChatGPT plans on mobile, web, and desktop. ChatGPT Work does not include restaurant reservation search.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>August 7, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>ChatGPT app experience updates</b></h2><p>We’ve made it easier to bring documents and saved files into your conversations, find what you need, and pick up where you left off.</p><h3>Writing, files, and search</h3><ul><li><p><b>Keep your formatting when you paste.</b> On the web, text copied from Google Docs or another ChatGPT conversation keeps its headings, bold text, links, and lists, both while you’re writing and after you send your message.</p></li><li><p><b>Add saved files without uploading them again.</b> Open the composer menu on the web, select <b>Add from library</b>, and choose a file you’ve already saved.</p></li><li><p><b>Select several Library items at once.</b> On the web, drag across multiple files or folders to select them together.</p></li><li><p><b>Find folders, files, and past conversations faster.</b> Search now includes folders and matches conversation titles across web, iOS, and Android. On the web, you can also search within Library to find your saved files.</p></li><li><p><b>Get answers that stay closer to your documents.</b> ChatGPT makes better use of information in attached files when you ask it to summarize a document, find specific details, review material, or draft from a source.</p></li><li><p><b>Use an updated composer when signed out.</b> The signed-out experience on the web now includes a cleaner, unified composer menu.</p></li></ul><h3><b>iOS and Android</b></h3><ul><li><p><b>Safeguards for background Voice conversations</b>. On mobile, ChatGPT can recognize when a background Voice conversation no longer seems active, check whether you’re still there, and end the session if you don’t respond. You can turn off Background conversations at any time in <b>Settings → Voice</b>.</p></li></ul><h3><b>iOS</b></h3><ul><li><p><b>Dictate with fewer interruptions.</b> If dictation fails because of a connection issue or timeout, ChatGPT automatically retries once, helping you finish recording without starting over.</p></li><li><p><b>Pick up completed answers without the replay.</b> When you return to a conversation, completed answers now appear without slowly replaying as though they’re still being generated.</p></li></ul><h3><b>Android</b></h3><ul><li><p><b>Start Voice conversations faster.</b> Improved connection times make it quicker to begin a Voice conversation with ChatGPT.</p></li></ul><h3><b>ChatGPT Work on the web</b></h3><ul><li><p><b>See your Work usage at a glance.</b> Eligible users on personal plans and ChatGPT Business can see their remaining Work usage in the sidebar. You can follow its progress, answer questions, change direction, and approve important actions as it works.</p><p>Work can also keep projects moving through Scheduled Tasks that run once, repeat on a schedule or trigger, or monitor for changes.</p><p>On web and mobile, Work is rolling out to paid plans except Free and Go. Evaluations showed gains in Japanese, Korean, Chinese, Urdu, Vietnamese, accented English, and long-form Spanish, French, Italian, and Portuguese.<br/><br/>Dictation is also more robust in noisy public spaces and workplace environments, with quiet or whispered speech, and when transcribing combinations of letters and numbers.<br/><br/>In our ChatGPT dictation evaluations, word error rate—a standard measure of transcription accuracy—was at least 10% lower for top languages tested than with the previous production model.</p><h2><b>Retiring GPT-4.5</b></h2><p>As of June 26, 2026, GPT-4.5 is no longer available in ChatGPT, including for custom GPTs. It also follows complex instructions more reliably: when a request includes several constraints or requirements, responses are more likely to address all of them and clearly explain why a recommendation is a good fit.<br/><br/>When users add constraints, clarify what they mean, or push back on an answer, the model should adapt more effectively instead of repeating its original approach.<br/><br/>Shopping and local business queries should also feel more useful. The model makes better use of location context to surface relevant nearby options and can bring together product recommendations, business information, and images more coherently when those elements are helpful.<br/><br/>Overall, responses should feel more tasteful and cohesive. Formatting is less templated and more intentionally designed, with greater creativity alongside better restraint.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>June 22, 2026</h1><h2><b>Large pastes are now handled as attachments for more plans</b></h2><p>Starting today for <b>ChatGPT Free and Go</b> users, long pastes are handled differently in ChatGPT.<br/><br/>If you paste <b>more than 10k characters</b> into the composer, ChatGPT will automatically convert the content into an <b>attachment</b> instead of inserting it directly into the text field. This keeps the composer cleaner and helps prevent large pastes from consuming your entire context window.<br/><br/>You can still move the content back into the message at any time by clicking on <b>Show in text field</b> to convert the attachment back to a direct paste.<br/><br/>This is already available to <b>ChatGPT Plus, Pro,</b> and <b>Business</b> users. Previously if you sent more than 5k characters it was sent as an attachment. The threshold has now also been raised to 10k characters.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>June 18, 2026</h1><h2><b>ChatGPT app experience updates</b></h2><p>ChatGPT now has more ways to help with common questions, from pronunciation guidance to World Cup updates. Ask about schedules, matchups, teams and players, country storylines, predictions, and what different match outcomes mean. Get started at <a href="https://chatgpt.com/football" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>chatgpt.com/football</u></a>.</p></li><li><p><b>App permission controls</b>: You can now choose when ChatGPT asks before using connected apps. Set ChatGPT to always ask, ask before making changes, or only ask before important changes. Manage preferences in Settings &gt; Apps.</p></li></ul><h3><b>Web</b></h3><ul><li><p><b>Easier pinning and sidebar organization</b>: It’s now easier to keep important work close in ChatGPT. You can pin chats and projects directly from the sidebar, and your Pinned section now includes both chats and projects. You can also organize Recents as one combined list, or group chats by project so related work stays together.</p></li><li><p><b>Easier conversation sharing</b>: Sharing a conversation is now faster, with a one-click flow that no longer opens a separate pop-up.</p></li><li><p><b>Create a note from a response</b>: Highlight text in a ChatGPT response and choose ‘Start writing’ to keep working on it, or save it to your Library to come back to later.</p></li></ul><h3><b>iOS</b></h3><ul><li><p><b>Improved camera and photo uploads</b>: Taking and uploading photos in ChatGPT on iOS is now faster and smoother, making it easier to add images to your conversations.</p></li></ul><h3><b>Android</b></h3><ul><li><p><b>Long-press send to choose a model</b>: Android users on paid plans can now long-press the send button to choose a model for a one-off message without changing their default model.</p></li><li><p><b>Updated composer and tools menu</b>: The composer in Android now has inline mentions, and an updated menu for Plugins.</p></li></ul><h2><b>Record &amp; Replay in Codex</b></h2><p>Record &amp; Replay is a new Codex app feature for macOS that lets eligible users demonstrate a workflow once and turn it into a reusable skill. It is useful for repetitive workflows or tasks that are easier to show than describe, such as creating a configured issue, publishing a video, or downloading a recurring report.<br/><br/>Record &amp; Replay requires Computer Use to be available and enabled, and initial availability excludes the European Union, UK and Switzerland. Learn more in the <a href="https://developers.openai.com/codex/record-and-replay" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Record &amp; Replay guide</u></a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>June 17, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>Scheduled tasks in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>With scheduled tasks, users can ask ChatGPT to send reminders, handle recurring work, or monitor things for them. This update makes tasks easier to find and manage, faster and more reliable, with more useful notifications.<br/><br/><br/><b>What’s changing</b></p><ul><li><p>A new Scheduled page, discoverable in the sidebar, gives users one place to view active tasks, see when they’ll run next, and pause, resume, edit, or delete them.</p></li><li><p>All tasks are faster and more reliable.</p></li><li><p>Users can schedule work for specific times or broader windows, such as morning, afternoon, or evening.</p></li><li><p>Monitoring tasks can search the web and check connected apps for changes and notify users only when there is something worth reporting.</p></li></ul><p><b>Availability and limits</b></p><ul><li><p>Rolling out to Plus, Pro, Business, and Enterprise users.</p></li><li><p>Active task limits vary by tier. </p></li><li><p>Tasks cannot run more than once per hour, and unattended tasks may automatically pause after a period of inactivity.</p></li></ul><p><b>Update on Pulse</b></p><p>Pulse is being sunset as proactive updates move into scheduled tasks. Pro users will continue to have access for 14 days from today. To keep receiving daily updates, you can ask ChatGPT to schedule a daily briefing based on your interests and past chats. </p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>June 12, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>Retiring GPT-5.2 models</b></h2><p>As of June 12, 2026, GPT-5.2 models are no longer available in ChatGPT.<br/><br/>This applies to GPT-5.2 Instant, GPT-5.2 Thinking, and GPT-5.2 Pro. Existing conversations that used GPT-5.2 will automatically continue on the corresponding GPT-5.5 model. GPT-5.5 models remain available in ChatGPT.<br/><br/>This retirement was previously announced with the release of GPT-5.3 Instant. Models will generally remain available in ChatGPT for 90 days after a successor is released.</p><h2><b>Additional controls for memory summary</b></h2><p>You can now delete the memories shown on your memory summary page and turn memory off by selecting “Delete and turn off memory” from the three-dot menu. This does not delete your past chats. If you turn memory back on later, ChatGPT may create new memories from chats that remain in your chat history. This control is available to all ChatGPT users on the web and rolling out on mobile soon.<br/><br/>To make changes to your memory summary, you can:</p><ul><li><p>Type what you want changed into the text box at the bottom of the memory summary, and it will update accordingly.</p></li><li><p>Highlight any text in the memory summary to make a specific correction.</p></li></ul><p>If you don’t want information from a chat to be used to personalize responses, you can turn off memory or use Temporary Chat. Temporary Chats don’t use existing memories or create new memories.<br/><br/>Memory updates, including memory summary, are still rolling out across ChatGPT plans and countries. You’ll be notified in ChatGPT when memory updates are available to you.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>June 11, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>Codex updates</b></h2><p>We’ve made several Codex updates, including:</p><ul><li><p>Eligible Plus and Pro users now have rate-limit reset banking, including one free reset at launch and the ability to send referral invitations from within the Codex app. When an eligible recipient sends their first Codex message, both people receive a banked rate-limit reset. Developer mode gives Codex controlled Chrome DevTools Protocol access so it can profile JavaScript, inspect console output and network traffic, review page state such as the DOM and applied styles, and diagnose live browser issues. Now it shows the preview right away so that it’s clear which photo you sent.</p></li><li><p><b>Cleaned up logged-out mobile web experience:</b> Logged-out users now see a composer in the middle of the screen to avoid being blocked by popups. This makes memory more useful by reducing stale or contradictory saved memories and helps ChatGPT better understand your preferences, goals, and ongoing work. You can review the memories that ChatGPT may use to personalize responses through sources or in your memory summary.<br/><br/>Memories are now updated automatically, with ChatGPT keeping track of the details it determines are most important so it can continue building on the context you’ve already shared. If you prefer to revert to the legacy saved memories system, go to Settings &gt; Memory &gt; Saved memories.<br/><br/>This update is rolling out to Plus and Pro users in the US today. To access it on iOS or Android, update your ChatGPT app to the latest version. We’ll expand to Free and Go plans and additional countries over the next few weeks. Users will receive an in-product notice when the update becomes available to them. For Plus and Pro users, ChatGPT can also remember more useful context, with twice as much memory capacity.</p><h2>Lockdown Mode is now available to all logged-in users</h2><p>Lockdown Mode is now available to all logged-in users across account types and workspaces. It is an optional opt-in advanced security setting that limits access to the web and external services to help reduce the risk of data exfiltration from prompt injection attacks.<br/><br/>When Lockdown Mode is on, ChatGPT restricts network-enabled capabilities such as live web browsing, deep research, agent mode, file downloads, and some web-derived image support. Personal users can turn it on from Settings &gt; Security. Workspace admins can configure access for members through workspace settings and role-based access controls.<br/><br/>Learn more: <a href="/en/articles/20001061" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Lockdown Mode</u></a></p><h2><b>Rolling out ads in the UK</b></h2><p>We&#x27;re beginning to roll out ads for users on Free and Go plans in the UK. Plus, Pro, Business, Enterprise, and Education plans will remain ad-free.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>June 2, 2026</h1><h2>Active account session controls</h2><p>We’re rolling out <b>Active sessions</b>, a new security feature in ChatGPT that helps users review sessions associated with their account and sign out of sessions they don’t recognize.<br/><br/>Users can now:</p><ul><li><p>Review first-party OpenAI sessions from <b>Settings</b> &gt; <b>Security</b> &gt; <b>Active sessions</b>, with available details such as device, app, approximate location, sign-in time, trusted-device status, and whether it is the current session</p></li><li><p>Log out of individual sessions or all sessions from <b>Active sessions</b></p></li></ul><p>Active sessions shows sessions known through session management, including ChatGPT, Codex, and API Platform sessions where available. lets you view spending, bills, subscriptions, net worth, and investment information in one place. From mobile, you can start or continue threads, answer questions, change direction, approve actions, review what Codex found, and move across connected hosts without stepping away from the work. That means you can keep an investigation moving, make a decision when Codex reaches a fork, or spin up a new idea before you are back at your desk. You can currently connect your phone to Codex running on macOS.<br/><br/>The mobile experience loads live context from the machine where Codex is operating, including project context, approvals, plugins, screenshots, terminal output, diffs, and test results. Codex in the ChatGPT mobile app is rolling out on iOS and Android across all plans, including Free and Go, in supported regions. To try it, update both the ChatGPT mobile app and the Codex app on macOS. Setup starts in the Codex App on the host and continues in ChatGPT after scanning a QR code; the host must remain awake, online, and running Codex for remote access to continue.<br/><br/>Learn more in <a href="https://developers.openai.com/codex/remote-connections" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Remote connections</a>.</p><h2><b>File library expands to Free and Go users, with storage management</b></h2><p>File library is expanding to Free and Go users, including to users in the European Economic Area (EEA) making it easier to find, reuse, and build on files you upload or create in ChatGPT. You can add recent files from the composer, browse saved files in Library, and ask ChatGPT about files you’ve used before, like “What should I know about the document I uploaded yesterday?”<br/><br/>We’re also introducing storage management across plans. Storage includes files you upload and files created in ChatGPT, including documents, spreadsheets, presentations, and images. Limits vary by plan:</p><ul><li><p><a href="/en/articles/9275245-chatgpt-free-tier-faq" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><b><u>Free</u></b></a><b>:</b> 500 MB</p></li><li><p><a href="/en/articles/11989085-what-is-chatgpt-go" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><b><u>Go</u></b></a><b>:</b> 4 GB</p></li><li><p><a href="/en/articles/6950777-what-is-chatgpt-plus" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><b><u>Plus</u></b></a><b> and </b><a href="/en/articles/8792828-what-is-chatgpt-business" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><b><u>Business</u></b></a><b>:</b> 20 GB</p></li><li><p><a href="/en/articles/9793128-what-is-chatgpt-pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><b><u>Pro</u></b></a><b>:</b> 100 GB </p></li></ul><p>Generated images will continue to appear in the Images tab.<br/><br/>Storage management is currently available on web and you can access it from Settings &gt; Storage; you can also delete files directly from Library.<br/><br/>Files remain in your Library until you delete them. Memory sources are only shown within your account experience, and if you share a chat, those sources are not shown in the shared chat. It also adds recovery keys, shorter active sessions, login notifications, and session management controls, giving users more protection and visibility across their account.<br/><br/>Because these protections are stricter, recovery is also stricter. Users should save their recovery keys and keep access to their enrolled passkeys or security keys. If access to all sign-in methods and recovery keys is lost, access to the account may be lost.</p><p>Learn more: <a href="/en/articles/20001221-advanced-account-security" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>Advanced Account Security</u></a></p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>April 28, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>Easier model selection in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>We’re making it easier to choose the right model before you send a message. Sharing your device location is completely optional and off until you choose to enable it. You can update device location sharing in Settings &gt; Data Controls at any time.<br/><br/>Enabling location sharing will also enable precise location. Precise location means ChatGPT can use your device’s specific location, such as an exact address, to provide more tailored results. For example, if you ask “what are the best coffee shops near me?”, ChatGPT can use your precise location to provide more relevant nearby results. On mobile devices, you can choose to toggle off precise location separately while keeping approximate device location sharing on for additional control.<br/><br/>ChatGPT deletes precise location data after it’s used to provide a more relevant response. If ChatGPT’s response includes information related to your specific location, such as the names of nearby restaurants or maps, that information becomes part of your conversation like any other response and will remain in your chat history unless you delete the conversation.<br/><br/>Parents who have set up parental controls can choose to disable device location sharing for their teen.<br/><br/>Rolling out to all ChatGPT consumer plans on iOS, Android, and web.</p><h2><b>Plugins in Codex</b></h2><p>Codex now includes a curated plugins directory that lets users discover, install, and use packaged workflows built from apps and skills directly in Codex. Auto top-up automatically replenishes your shared credit balance when it falls below the minimum balance you choose, using your default payment method on file to purchase only the amount needed to reach your target balance.</p><p>You can manage auto top-up from Codex Settings → Usage Dashboard. Auto top-up applies to the same shared credit balance used across supported features like Codex and Sora, and if your balance is already below your selected minimum when you turn it on, a top-up may happen right away. Learn more: <a href="/en/articles/12642688-using-credits-for-flexible-usage-in-chatgpt-freegopluspro-sora" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Using Credits for Flexible Usage in ChatGPT (Free/Go/Plus/Pro) &amp; Sora</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>March 5, 2026</b></h1><h2><b>GPT-5.4 Thinking in ChatGPT</b></h2><p>GPT‑5.4 brings together the best of our recent advances in reasoning, coding, and agentic workflows into a single frontier model. It incorporates the industry-leading coding capabilities of <a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-5-3-codex/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">GPT‑5.3‑Codex⁠</a> while improving how the model works across tools, software environments, and professional tasks involving spreadsheets, presentations, and documents. The result is a model that gets complex real work done accurately, effectively, and efficiently—delivering what you asked for with less back and forth.<br/><br/>In ChatGPT, GPT‑5.4 Thinking can now provide an upfront plan of its thinking, so you can <b>adjust course mid-response</b> while it’s working, and arrive at a final output that’s more closely aligned with what you need without additional turns. GPT‑5.4 Thinking improves <b>deep web research,</b> particularly for highly specific queries, while <b>better maintaining context</b> for questions that require longer thinking. GPT‑5.4 Thinking also has improved context window management that supports its ability to think for longer. Together, these improvements mean higher-quality answers that arrive faster and stay relevant to the task at hand.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>March 4, 2026</h1><h2><b>Codex app on Windows</b></h2><p>The Codex app is now available on Windows for ChatGPT plans that include Codex. Plus, Pro, Business, Enterprise, and Education plans do not have ads. The test will be a subset of users to start. We will expand over the coming weeks as we learn from this test.<br/><br/>ChatGPT’s answers are generated independently from advertising. Answers are optimized based on what’s most helpful to you. When you see an ad, they are always clearly labeled as sponsored and visually separated from the organic answer.<br/><br/>Ads are shown using information that stays within ChatGPT and is not shared with advertisers. This includes what you’re discussing, how you interact with ads (such as ads you hide or engage with), and your past chats and memories, if you have ads personalization enabled. You’re in control of this experience: you can dismiss ads, share feedback, learn how and why you’re being shown an ad, delete your ad data with one tap, and manage ad personalization at any time.<br/><br/>Advertisers never see your chats or personal details and receive only aggregated performance information, like impressions and clicks. During the test, ads do not appear in chats about sensitive topics or regulated topics, including health, mental health, or politics, and are not shown to users who tell us or we predict are under 18.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>February 4, 2026</h1><h2><b>Update on thinking time settings for GPT-5.2 Thinking in ChatGPT</b></h2><p><b>Jan 10, 2026:</b> We lowered the Standard and Light thinking time as we observed users prefer faster responses. As part of this update, the <b>Extended</b> thinking setting for GPT-5.2 was unintentionally changed to be lower which we have now fixed.<br/><br/><br/><b>February 3, 2026:</b> We made another small reduction to Standard thinking time based on testing.<br/><br/><br/><b>February 4, 2026:</b> We’re restoring the <b>Extended</b> thinking level for GPT-5.2 Thinking to its prior setting, correcting the inadvertent reduction from January. Extended is now back to its prior level.<br/><br/>We periodically adjust the default thinking time for our reasoning models. These changes are based on ongoing experiments to find the best balance between answer quality and response speed for users.<br/><br/>The <a href="https://x.com/OpenAI/status/1968395215536042241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">thinking level toggle</a> introduced in September 2025 gives users more choice beyond Standard, allowing them to select the right thinking level for their question—whether they want lighter, faster responses or more extended reasoning when depth and accuracy matter more.<br/><br/>Thinking time is not directly comparable across different models. Each model is tuned independently to what works best for users. We’ll continue to adjust these settings as models evolve, and will keep giving users clear controls when there are meaningful tradeoffs to choose from.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>February 2, 2026</h1><h2><b>Introducing the Codex app</b></h2><p>Today we’re releasing the Codex app for macOS,  a command center for managing multiple coding agents in parallel. GPT-5.2 is smarter and more useful for both work and learning—while remaining just as enjoyable to talk to. As with GPT-5.1, we’re continuing to improve our main models for everyone on a regular basis to make them more useful and enjoyable to use.<br/><br/><br/><b>GPT-5.2 Instant</b> is a fast yet powerful workhorse for everyday work and learning, with clear improvements in info-seeking questions, how-tos and walk-throughs, technical writing, and translation, all while retaining the warmer, more conversational tone introduced in GPT-5.1 Instant. Early testers noted its more clearly structured explanations where important info is highlighted upfront. It’s also more effective at supporting studying and skill-building, as well as offering clearer job and career guidance.<br/><br/><br/><b>GPT-5.2 Thinking</b> solves harder work tasks more effectively and with more polish—particularly in spreadsheet formatting and financial modeling, alongside improvements in slideshow creation. Early testing showed gains in coding, summarizing long documents, answering questions about uploaded files, walking through complex math and logic step by step, and helping with planning and decision-making with clearer structure and thoughtful detail.<br/><br/><br/><b>GPT-5.2 Pro</b> is our smartest and most trustworthy model yet for difficult questions where a higher-quality answer is worth the wait. In early testing, it shows fewer major errors and stronger performance across complex domains like programming.<br/><br/>All three models (Instant, Thinking, and Pro) have a new knowledge cutoff of August 2025. For users, this means GPT-5.2 starts with a more current understanding of the world, so answers are more accurate and useful, with more relevant examples and context, even before turning to web search.<br/><br/>Read our <a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-5-2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><u>blog</u></a> for more details on examples and evals.<br/><br/><br/><b>Update for free and Go users:</b> We’re removing automatic model switching for reasoning in ChatGPT. Previously, some questions were automatically routed to the Thinking model when ChatGPT determined it might help. To maximize choice, free users will now use <b>GPT-5.2 Instant</b> by default, and can still choose to use reasoning anytime by selecting <b>Thinking</b> from the tools menu in the message composer.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>November 25, 2025</h1><h2>An improved Voice interface: the audio you know, with text and visuals when you need them.</h2><p>We’re making ChatGPT Voice a seamless part of the ChatGPT experience, so you can use voice right inside the chat interface you already use every day—no separate mode required.<br/><br/>Many of you have asked for a more flexible Voice experience—now, you can speak, listen, and see your conversation at the same time. Answers will be spoken alongside streamed text, image search results, widgets (maps, weather, etc.) and more—right in the same chat thread.<br/><br/>This makes voice chats clearer, richer and more contextual by pairing spoken answers with visual context. These updates were guided by mental health experts, and help ChatGPT de-escalate conversations and point people to real-world crisis resources when appropriate, while still using language that feels supportive and grounding.<br/><br/>As we shared in a <a href="https://openai.com/index/building-more-helpful-chatgpt-experiences-for-everyone/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><b>recent blog</b></a>, we&#x27;ve been using our real-time router to direct sensitive parts of conversations—such as those showing signs of acute distress—to reasoning models. GPT-5 Instant now performs just as well as GPT-5 Thinking on these types of questions. When GPT-5 Auto or a non-reasoning model is selected, we&#x27;ll instead route these conversations to GPT-5 Instant to more quickly provide helpful and beneficial responses. ChatGPT will continue to tell users which model is active when asked.<br/><br/><br/><b>This update to GPT-5 Instant is starting to roll out to ChatGPT users today.</b> We’re continuing to work on improvements and will keep updating the model to make it smarter and safer over time.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 29, 2025</h1><h2>Parental Controls</h2><p>We’re rolling out parental controls globally in ChatGPT to help families guide how ChatGPT works in their homes. Available starting on the web today, mobile soon.<br/><br/>To set up parental controls, a parent or guardian sends an invite to their teen to connect accounts. After the teen accepts, the parent can manage the teen’s settings from their own account. Teens can also invite a parent to connect.<br/><br/>Once a teen is connected to a parent’s account, they automatically receive additional content protections, such as reduced graphic content and viral challenges. Parents will have the option to turn this setting off if they choose, but teen users cannot make changes.<br/><br/>Parents don’t have access to their teen’s conversations, except in rare cases where our system and trained reviewers detect possible signs of serious safety risk, parents may be notified — but only with the information needed to support their teen’s safety. Parents can choose if they’d like to be contacted by email, SMS, push notification, or all three.<br/><br/>Parents can adjust certain features, set time limits, and add safeguards that work for their family including:</p><ul><li><p><b>Set quiet hours</b>, or specific times when ChatGPT can’t be used.</p></li><li><p><b>Turn off voice mode</b>, to remove the option to use voice mode in ChatGPT.</p></li><li><p><b>Turn off memory</b>, so ChatGPT won’t save and use memories when responding.</p></li><li><p><b>Remove image generation</b>, so ChatGPT won’t have the ability to create or edit images.</p></li><li><p><b>Opt out of model training,</b> so their teen’s conversations won’t be used to improve models powering ChatGPT and Sora.</p></li></ul><h2>Introducing Instant Checkout and the Agentic Commerce Protocol</h2><p>We’re introducing Instant Checkout in ChatGPT and the Agentic Commerce Protocol that powers it, built with Stripe.<br/><br/>ChatGPT can help you find anything on the web and now with Instant Checkout, you can seamlessly buy directly from the merchant, right in chat. Starting today, U.S. ChatGPT Plus, Pro, and Free users can buy directly from U.S. Etsy sellers through ChatGPT, with over 1 million Shopify merchants, like Glossier, SKIMS, Spanx and Vuori, coming soon.</p><h3>Instant Checkout</h3><p>Instant Checkout makes it easy to go from chat to checkout in just a few taps.</p><ul><li><p>Ask ChatGPT a shopping question and it finds organic, unsponsored product results from across the web.</p></li><li><p>Choose an item and ChatGPT shows where to buy, ranking merchants by factors like inventory, price, quality, primary seller status, and Instant Checkout availability.</p></li><li><p>If Instant Checkout is offered, tap Buy, confirm shipping and payment details, and pay in one tap with your card on file or other express pay options.</p></li><li><p>If Instant Checkout isn’t available, ChatGPT provides a direct link so you can purchase from the merchant’s site.</p></li></ul><p>You’re in control at all times and give explicit confirmation to purchase. Payment goes directly from you to the merchant, not through OpenAI. <a href="/en/articles/12440090-instant-checkout" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about Instant Checkout</a>.</p><h3>Agentic Commerce Protocol</h3><p>The Agentic Commerce Protocol (ACP) gives merchants and developers a way to build agentic commerce experiences that work with their current systems and connect them with 700M+ weekly active users on ChatGPT.<br/><br/>If you’re a merchant and want to add Instant Checkout in ChatGPT, apply on our<a href="http://chatgpt.com/merchants" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"> merchant page.</a> We’ll be accepting and onboarding merchants on a rolling basis.<br/><br/>Developers can start exploring the Agentic Commerce Protocol <a href="https://developers.openai.com/commerce" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">documentation</a> and example implementations on <a href="https://github.com/agentic-commerce-protocol/agentic-commerce-protocol" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">GitHub</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 25, 2025</h1><h2>Introducing ChatGPT Pulse for Pro users</h2><p>We’re launching an early preview of ChatGPT pulse for <b>Pro users on iOS and Android</b>. This is a new experience where ChatGPT can now proactively deliver personalized, focused updates for you once a day. These updates are based on asynchronous research ChatGPT can do on your behalf overnight, synthesizing information from your memory, chat history, and direct feedback to learn what’s most relevant to you.<br/><br/>Updates are delivered once a day as visual summaries you can scan at a glance or dive deeper into. Each topic opens into more detail where you can ask follow-up questions. You can shape what shows up in pulse with a quick thumbs up or down, or by using curate to tell ChatGPT what you’d like to see.<br/><br/>If you ask for something new in the evening, we aim to include it the next morning, though exact timing may vary. Pulse refreshes daily, and you can save any item into a chat to keep it.<br/><br/>As a preview, pulse has some limitations. It aims to show you what’s most relevant and useful but you may still see suggestions that miss the mark. For example, you may get tips for a project you already completed. You can guide what shows up by telling ChatGPT directly. It remembers your feedback for next time and improves as it learns from real use.<br/><br/>It requires saved memory and chat history to be turned on, and you can switch pulse off anytime in settings. See <a href="/en/articles/12293630-chatgpt-pulse" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ChatGPT Pulse</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 22, 2025</h1><h2>ChatGPT Go now available in Indonesia</h2><p>We&#x27;re launching ChatGPT Go, our low cost subscription plan, in Indonesia.<br/><br/>For Rp 75.000/month, ChatGPT Go provides everything included in the <a href="/en/articles/9275245-chatgpt-free-tier-faq" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Free</a> plan, and:</p><ul><li><p>More messages, larger file uploads, and expanded image generation</p></li><li><p>Access to advanced data analysis</p></li><li><p>Longer memory for more personalized responses</p></li></ul><p>Available on web, mobile (iOS &amp; Android), and desktop (macOS &amp; Windows).<br/><br/>Learn more about <a href="/en/articles/11989085-what-is-chatgpt-go" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ChatGPT Go</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 18, 2025</h1><h2>Voice Updates</h2><p>We’re improving the quality and latency of responses of the version of Advanced Voice powered by GPT-4o mini.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 17, 2025</h1><h2>Toggle thinking time in the message composer</h2><h3>What’s new</h3><p>We’re giving Plus, Business, and Pro users more control over how long GPT-5 thinks before responding. You can now choose the level of reasoning time, so you can decide whether you want a quicker answer or more in-depth reasoning for your task.</p><h3>How it works</h3><p>When you select <b>GPT-5 with Thinking</b> in ChatGPT, you’ll see a new <b>thinking time</b> toggle in the message composer.</p><ul><li><p><b>Standard</b> (new default, balancing speed and intelligence) and <b>Extended</b> (the previous default for Plus) are available to all <b>Plus, Business</b> users</p></li><li><p><b>Pro</b> users get two additional options: <b>Light</b> (snappiest) and <b>Heavy</b> (deeper reasoning).</p></li><li><p>All queries default to <b>Standard</b> unless you choose to change it. Once you choose a different thinking time, your preference is saved and used for future queries until you switch again.</p></li></ul><h3>Why we’re making this change</h3><p>We’ve heard from some of you that GPT-5 Thinking’s answers can take longer than you’d like. Now you can decide what you need in the moment:</p><ul><li><p><b>Light / Standard</b> → quicker answers that still apply reasoning, balancing speed and intelligence.</p></li><li><p><b>Extended / Heavy</b> → more time for deeper, more comprehensive responses when the stakes are higher or the question is more difficult.</p></li></ul><h3>What’s next</h3><p>The thinking-time toggle is live today on chatgpt.com. Settings you choose on the web won’t yet sync to mobile, but we’ll bring this control to our iOS and Android apps in the coming weeks.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 16, 2025</h1><h2>Improvements to search in ChatGPT</h2><p>We’ve made further improvements to search in ChatGPT, making results more accurate, reliable, and useful.</p><ul><li><p><b>Factuality:</b> Fewer hallucinations, improving answer quality.</p></li><li><p><b>Shopping:</b> Search is better at detecting shopping intent, showing products when you want them and keeping results focused when you don’t.</p></li><li><p><b>Formatting</b>: Answers are better formatted for quick understanding without losing detail or quality.</p></li></ul></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>September 15, 2025</h1><h2>Personalization settings are now in Settings</h2><p>Personalization no longer opens in a separate modal. is now available. When creating a project, users have the option to enable <b>project-only memory.</b><br/><br/>With <b>project-only memory</b> enabled, ChatGPT can use other conversations in that project for additional context, and won’t use your <a href="/en/articles/11146739-how-does-reference-saved-memories-work" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">saved memories</a> from outside the project to shape responses. Additionally, it won’t carry anything from the project into future chats outside of the project.<br/><br/>This creates a focused, self-contained space, which is useful for long-running or sensitive work where you want ChatGPT to stay anchored to that project’s tone, context, and history.<br/><br/><br/><b>Note:</b></p><ul><li><p><b>Personal Memory</b> must be enabled to utilize this feature.</p><ul><li><p><b>Settings</b> -&gt; <b>Personalization</b> -&gt; <b>Memory</b></p></li></ul></li><li><p>This feature will initially only be available on the ChatGPT website and Windows app. Support for mobile (iOS and Android) and macOS app will follow in the coming weeks.</p></li></ul><p>Learn more about <a href="/en/articles/10169521-projects-in-chatgpt#h_374a3efb05" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">memory in projects</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>August 18, 2025</h1><h2>Introducing ChatGPT Go</h2><p>We’re launching ChatGPT Go, a new low-cost subscription plan. This is in response to user feedback that the initial version of GPT-5 came across as too reserved and professional. The differences in personality should feel subtle but create a noticeably more approachable ChatGPT experience.<br/><br/>Warmth here means small acknowledgements that make interactions feel more personable — for example, “Good question,” “Great start,” or briefly recognizing the user’s circumstances when relevant. This is different from sycophancy, which we define as excessive flattery that can feel insincere. You can find more about our learnings on reducing sycophancy <a href="https://openai.com/index/expanding-on-sycophancy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">here</a>.<br/><br/>This update does not show an increase in sycophancy compared to the previous personality of GPT-5, measured by the same internal evals we ran and detailed in our <a href="https://cdn.openai.com/pdf/8124a3ce-ab78-4f06-96eb-49ea29ffb52f/gpt5-system-card-aug7.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">system card</a>. Teaching models to be warm without being sycophantic is an ongoing research challenge, and we will continue iterating to get this right. More updates are coming soon.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>August 13, 2025</h1><h2>Gmail, Google Calendar, and Google Contacts Connectors in ChatGPT (Plus)</h2><p>Gmail, Google Calendar, and Google Contacts Connectors in ChatGPT are now available to Plus users globally.
