In testing, we found users preferred these search improvements over our previous search experience.</p><h3>Improved quality</h3><ul><li><p>Smarter responses that are more intelligent, are better at understanding what you’re asking, and provide more comprehensive answers.</p></li><li><p>Handles longer conversational contexts, allowing better intelligence in longer conversations.</p></li></ul><h3>Improved search capability and instruction following</h3><ul><li><p>More robust ability to follow instructions, especially in longer conversations, significantly reducing repetitive responses.</p></li><li><p>Capability to run multiple searches automatically for complex or difficult questions.</p></li><li><p>Search the web using an image you’ve uploaded.</p></li></ul><h3>Known limitations</h3><ul><li><p>Users may notice longer responses with this new search experience.</p></li><li><p>In some cases, a “chain of thought” reasoning will show up unexpectedly for simple queries. A fix for this is rolling out to users shortly.</p></li><li><p>ChatGPT may still make occasional mistakes - please double check responses.</p></li></ul></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>June 12, 2025</h1><h2>Expanded Model Support for Custom GPTs</h2><p>Creators can now choose from the full set of ChatGPT models (GPT-4o, o3, o4-mini and more) when building Custom GPTs—making it easier to fine-tune performance for different tasks, industries, and workflows. Creators can also set a recommended model to guide users.<br/><br/>Key details:</p><ul><li><p>GPTs <em>without Custom Actions</em> can use the model picker to select from all models available to the user.</p></li><li><p>GPTs <em>with Custom Actions</em> currently support GPT-4o and 4.1</p></li><li><p>Available on <b>web</b> for users on <b>Plus, Pro and Team</b> plans.</p></li><li><p>Enterprise and Edu rollout coming soon.</p></li></ul><div class="intercom-container intercom-align-center"><img src="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-42c382e8919222ca76fb3a1a/11e141f2c5d0a0d45437e44cf726bfdc/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344" width="2652" height="412" srcSet="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-42c382e8919222ca76fb3a1a/11e141f2c5d0a0d45437e44cf726bfdc/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=320 320w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-42c382e8919222ca76fb3a1a/11e141f2c5d0a0d45437e44cf726bfdc/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=480 480w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-42c382e8919222ca76fb3a1a/11e141f2c5d0a0d45437e44cf726bfdc/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=672 672w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-42c382e8919222ca76fb3a1a/11e141f2c5d0a0d45437e44cf726bfdc/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=960 960w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-42c382e8919222ca76fb3a1a/11e141f2c5d0a0d45437e44cf726bfdc/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344 1344w" sizes="(min-width: 792px) 672px, (min-width: 640px) calc(100vw - 120px), calc(100vw - 40px)" alt="Recommended Model setting with o3 selected as the default model" decoding="async" loading="lazy"/></div><h2>Adding More Capabilities to Projects</h2><p>Starting today, we’re adding several updates to projects in ChatGPT to help you do more focused work. These updates are available for Plus, Pro, and Team users.</p><ul><li><p>Deep research and voice mode support</p></li><li><p>Improvements to memory to reference past chats in a project*</p></li><li><p>Sharing chats from projects</p></li><li><p>Starting a new project directly from a chat</p></li><li><p>Upload files and access model selector on mobile</p></li></ul><p><a href="/en/articles/10169521-using-projects-in-chatgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more</a> about projects.<br/><br/>*Memory improvements are available for Plus and Pro users.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>June 10, 2025</h1><h2>Today, we&#x27;re launching OpenAI o3-pro—available now for Pro users in ChatGPT and in our API.</h2><p>Like o1-pro, o3-pro is a version of our most intelligent model, o3, designed to think longer and provide the most reliable responses. Since the launch of o1-pro, users have favored this model for domains such as math, science, and coding—areas where o3-pro continues to excel, as shown in academic evaluations. Like o3, o3-pro has access to tools that make ChatGPT useful—it can search the web, analyze files, reason about visual inputs, use Python, personalize responses using memory, and more. Because o3-pro has access to tools, responses typically take longer than o1-pro to complete. We recommend using it for challenging questions where reliability matters more than speed, and waiting a few minutes is worth the tradeoff.<br/><br/>In expert evaluations, reviewers consistently prefer o3-pro over o3 in every tested category and especially in key domains like science, education, programming, business, and writing help. Reviewers also rated o3-pro consistently higher for clarity, comprehensiveness, instruction-following, and accuracy.</p><div class="intercom-container intercom-align-center"><img src="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-e5b17caf2d426949b0749916/bd6e50fc647b699b51a6dd84c2f81eb6/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344" width="3840" height="2160" srcSet="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-e5b17caf2d426949b0749916/bd6e50fc647b699b51a6dd84c2f81eb6/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=320 320w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-e5b17caf2d426949b0749916/bd6e50fc647b699b51a6dd84c2f81eb6/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=480 480w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-e5b17caf2d426949b0749916/bd6e50fc647b699b51a6dd84c2f81eb6/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=672 672w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-e5b17caf2d426949b0749916/bd6e50fc647b699b51a6dd84c2f81eb6/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=960 960w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-e5b17caf2d426949b0749916/bd6e50fc647b699b51a6dd84c2f81eb6/image.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344 1344w" sizes="(min-width: 792px) 672px, (min-width: 640px) calc(100vw - When we first launched Advanced Voice, it represented a leap forward in AI speech—now, it speaks even more naturally, with subtler intonation, realistic cadence (including pauses and emphases), and more on-point expressiveness for certain emotions including empathy, sarcasm, and more.<br/><br/>Voice also now offers intuitive and effective language translation. Just ask Voice to translate between languages, and it will continue translating throughout your conversation until you tell it to stop or switch. It’s ready to translate whenever you need it—whether you&#x27;re asking for directions in Italy or chatting with a colleague from the Tokyo office. For example, at a restaurant in Brazil, Voice can translate your English sentences into Portuguese, and the waiter’s Portuguese responses back into English—making conversations effortless, no matter where you are or who you&#x27;re speaking with.<br/><br/>This upgrade to Advanced Voice is available for all paid users across markets and platforms—just tap the Voice icon in the message composer to get started.<br/><br/>This update is in addition to improvements we made earlier this year to ensure fewer interruptions and improved accents.</p><h3>Known Limitations</h3><p>In testing, we&#x27;ve observed that this update may occasionally cause minor decreases in audio quality, including unexpected variations in tone and pitch. These issues are more noticeable with certain voice options. We expect to improve audio consistency over time.<br/><br/>Additionally, rare hallucinations in Voice Mode persist with this update, resulting in unintended sounds resembling ads, gibberish, or background music. We are actively investigating these issues and working toward a solution.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>June 4, 2025</h1><h2>Connectors in beta for deep research (Plus, Pro, Team, Enterprise, Edu)</h2><p>ChatGPT Team, Enterprise, and Edu customers globally can use connectors in deep research, as well as Pro and Plus users (excluding users in Switzerland, EEA, and the UK) to generate long-form, cited responses that include your company’s internal tools.</p><ul><li><p>Supported connectors: Google Drive, SharePoint, Dropbox, Box, Outlook, Gmail, Google Calendar, Linear, GitHub, HubSpot, and Teams</p></li><li><p>Combines internal + web sources for synthesis</p></li></ul><p>Learn more about <a href="/en/articles/11487775" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Connectors in ChatGPT</a>.</p><h2>Custom connectors via Model Context Protocol (Pro, Team, Enterprise, Edu)</h2><p>Admins and users can now build and deploy custom connectors to proprietary systems using Model Context Protocol (MCP).</p><ul><li><p>Requires a remote MCP server</p></li><li><p>Available only in deep research</p></li><li><p>Admin-published connectors appear in the connector list for all users</p></li></ul><p>Learn more about <a href="http://platform.openai.com/docs/mcp" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">building custom connectors with MCP</a>. Starting today, GPT-4.1 mini replaces GPT-4o mini in the model picker under &quot;more models&quot; for paid users, and will serve as the fallback model for free users once they reach their GPT-4o usage limits. tapping that button opens a bottom‑sheet menu where users can choose tools like Create an image or Search the web.</p><div class="intercom-container intercom-align-center"><img src="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-5affc4bc052d8e7034e240fc/0a19be2b1c650cab5eaecd91143d9ab2/image__281_29.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344" width="2628" height="710" srcSet="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-5affc4bc052d8e7034e240fc/0a19be2b1c650cab5eaecd91143d9ab2/image__281_29.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=320 320w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-5affc4bc052d8e7034e240fc/0a19be2b1c650cab5eaecd91143d9ab2/image__281_29.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=480 480w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-5affc4bc052d8e7034e240fc/0a19be2b1c650cab5eaecd91143d9ab2/image__281_29.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=672 672w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-5affc4bc052d8e7034e240fc/0a19be2b1c650cab5eaecd91143d9ab2/image__281_29.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=960 960w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-5affc4bc052d8e7034e240fc/0a19be2b1c650cab5eaecd91143d9ab2/image__281_29.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344 1344w" sizes="(min-width: 792px) 672px, (min-width: 640px) calc(100vw - 120px), calc(100vw - 40px)" alt="ChatGPT mobile Ask anything composer with add, tools, microphone, and voice mode buttons" decoding="async" loading="lazy"/></div><p>No tools are deprecated—access is simply consolidated to clear space and reduce on‑screen clutter.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>May 1, 2025</h1><h2>Sunsetting Monday GPT</h2><p>Launched on April 1 as a one-month surprise, our “Monday” personality (available in both voice and text) has now been sunset. Please stay tuned for what’s next!</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>April 29, 2025</h1><h2>Updates to GPT-4o</h2><p>We&#x27;ve reverted the most recent update to GPT-4o due to issues with overly agreeable responses (sycophancy).<br/><br/>We’re actively working on further improvements. For more details, check out our <a href="https://openai.com/index/sycophancy-in-gpt-4o/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">blog post</a> explaining what happened and our initial findings, and <a href="https://openai.com/index/expanding-on-sycophancy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">this blog post</a> where we expand on what we missed with sycophancy and the changes we&#x27;re going to make going forward.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>April 25, 2025</h1><h2>Improvements to GPT-4o</h2><p>We’re making additional improvements to GPT-4o, optimizing when it saves memories and enhancing problem-solving capabilities for STEM. We’ve also made subtle changes to the way it responds, making it more proactive and better at guiding conversations toward productive outcomes. We think these updates help GPT-4o feel more intuitive and effective across a variety of tasks–we hope you agree!</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>April 16, 2025</h1><h2>o3 and o4-mini in ChatGPT</h2><p>Today, we’re releasing OpenAI <b>o3</b> and <b>o4-mini,</b> the latest in our o-series of models trained to think for longer before responding. These are the smartest models we’ve released to date, representing a step change in ChatGPT&#x27;s capabilities for everyone from curious users to advanced researchers. For the first time, our reasoning models can agentically use and combine every tool within ChatGPT—this includes searching the web, analyzing uploaded files and other data with Python, reasoning deeply about visual inputs, and even generating images. Critically, these models are trained to reason about when and how to use tools to produce detailed and thoughtful answers in the right output formats, typically in under a minute, to solve more complex problems. This allows them to tackle multi-faceted questions more effectively, a step toward a more agentic ChatGPT that can independently execute tasks on your behalf. The combined power of state-of-the-art reasoning with full tool access translates into significantly stronger performance across academic benchmarks and real-world tasks, setting a new standard in both intelligence and usefulness.</p><h2>Memory with Search</h2><p>ChatGPT can also use memories to inform search queries when ChatGPT searches the web using third-party search providers. <a href="/en/articles/9237897-chatgpt-search" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Learn more about search</a>.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>April 15, 2025</h1><h2>ChatGPT Image Library</h2><p>All images you create with ChatGPT are now automatically saved to a new <a href="/en/articles/11084440-chatgpt-image-library" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Library</a> in the sidebar, giving you one place to browse, revisit, and reuse your work without digging through past conversations. The Library is rolling out today on Web, iOS, and Android for Free, Plus, and Pro users (Enterprise / Edu support coming soon). For now, it displays images generated with 4o Image Generation while we backfill older creations, and you can remove an image by deleting the conversation where it was made.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>April 10, 2025</h1><h3>Sunsetting GPT‑4 in ChatGPT</h3><p><em>Effective  April  30,  2025, GPT‑4 will be retired from ChatGPT and fully replaced by GPT‑4o.</em><br/><br/>GPT‑4o is our newer, natively multimodal model. In head‑to‑head evaluations it consistently surpasses GPT‑4 in writing, coding, STEM, and more.<br/><br/>Recent upgrades have further improved GPT‑4o’s instruction following, problem solving, and conversational flow, making it a natural successor to GPT‑4.<br/><br/>GPT-4 will still be available in the API.<br/><br/>GPT‑4 marked a pivotal moment in ChatGPT’s evolution. We’re grateful for the breakthroughs it enabled and for the feedback that helped shape its successor. GPT‑4o builds on that foundation to deliver even greater capability, consistency, and creativity.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>March 27, 2025</h1><h2>Improvements to GPT-4o</h2><p>We’ve made improvements to GPT-4o—it now feels more intuitive, creative, and collaborative, with enhanced instruction-following, smarter coding capabilities, and a clearer communication style.</p><h3>Smarter problem-solving in STEM and coding:</h3><p>GPT-4o has further improved its capability to tackle complex technical and coding problems. It now generates cleaner, simpler frontend code, more accurately thinks through existing code to identify necessary changes, and consistently produces coding outputs that successfully compile and run, streamlining your coding workflows.</p><h3>Enhanced instruction-following and formatting accuracy:</h3><p>GPT-4o is now more adept at following detailed instructions, especially for prompts containing multiple or complex requests. It improves on generating outputs according to the format requested and achieves higher accuracy in classification tasks.</p><h3>“Fuzzy” improvements:</h3><p>Early testers say that the model seems to better understand the implied intent behind their prompts, especially when it comes to creative and collaborative tasks. It’s also slightly more concise and clear, using fewer markdown hierarchies and emojis for responses that are easier to read, less cluttered, and more focused. We&#x27;re curious to see if our users also find this to be the case.<br/><br/>This model is now available in ChatGPT and in the API as the newest snapshot of chatgpt-4o-latest. We plan to bring these improvements to a dated model in the API in the coming weeks.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>March 18, 2025</h1><p>We have been working on a variety of usability and performance improvements and bug fixes across Web, Android, and iOS. You can set custom instructions and upload files in your Projects, and any conversations in your Project share context with your uploaded files and custom instructions. Chats in Projects use GPT-4o and support the following features:</p><ul><li><p>Canvas</p></li><li><p>Advanced data analysis</p></li><li><p>DALL•E</p></li><li><p>Search</p></li></ul><p>ChatGPT Projects are available to all Plus, Team, and Pro users.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1><b>Dec 12, 2024</b></h1><h2><b>Santa Voice and Video and Screen Share in Voice Chats</b></h2><p>Today we started rolling out two updates to voice mode in ChatGPT:</p><ul><li><p><b>Santa voice:</b> You can now chat with Santa in ChatGPT! Until early January, you can find Santa in the list of available voices in settings or by tapping on a snowflake icon. We are rolling out Santa globally to all logged in users today, and you can chat with Santa everywhere you use voice mode.</p></li></ul><p>The first time you chat with Santa, your advanced voice limit will reset one time. So, if you’ve already used up your advanced voice limit for the day or the month (based on your subscription plan), you can still chat with Santa for the first time using advanced voice.</p><div class="intercom-container intercom-align-center"><img src="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-cdaecdf9bcdaa1448f5b5674/2aa198a4a88b028374402cd7a3147b8b/AD_4nXc6Yrl0VAWKWtH0GECNN-FtImdMfgdYXUyrpLVWkA0wLQOlZZ1BnPAF_YhopSPdRZ7B6LwpvwgfoTUBzZXVl5_-L5WpBAYec6oVE4-0-XJ_PPUkx24m3LpAgUAy?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=498" width="498" height="710" srcSet="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-cdaecdf9bcdaa1448f5b5674/2aa198a4a88b028374402cd7a3147b8b/AD_4nXc6Yrl0VAWKWtH0GECNN-FtImdMfgdYXUyrpLVWkA0wLQOlZZ1BnPAF_YhopSPdRZ7B6LwpvwgfoTUBzZXVl5_-L5WpBAYec6oVE4-0-XJ_PPUkx24m3LpAgUAy?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=320 320w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-cdaecdf9bcdaa1448f5b5674/2aa198a4a88b028374402cd7a3147b8b/AD_4nXc6Yrl0VAWKWtH0GECNN-FtImdMfgdYXUyrpLVWkA0wLQOlZZ1BnPAF_YhopSPdRZ7B6LwpvwgfoTUBzZXVl5_-L5WpBAYec6oVE4-0-XJ_PPUkx24m3LpAgUAy?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=480 480w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-cdaecdf9bcdaa1448f5b5674/2aa198a4a88b028374402cd7a3147b8b/AD_4nXc6Yrl0VAWKWtH0GECNN-FtImdMfgdYXUyrpLVWkA0wLQOlZZ1BnPAF_YhopSPdRZ7B6LwpvwgfoTUBzZXVl5_-L5WpBAYec6oVE4-0-XJ_PPUkx24m3LpAgUAy?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=498 498w" sizes="(min-width: 792px) 672px, (min-width: 640px) calc(100vw - 120px), calc(100vw - 40px)" alt="Santa theme card with snowy blue artwork and the subtitle Merry and bright" decoding="async" loading="lazy"/></div><ul><li><p><b>Video and screenshare:</b> In our latest mobile apps, we are starting to roll out real-time video, screen share, and image upload capabilities in advanced voice. We expect to complete this rollout to all Team and most Plus and Pro users, except for those in the European Union, Switzerland, Iceland, Norway, and Liechtenstein, over the next week. Video and screen share are subject to daily usage limits for all plans, and image uploads are subject to ChatGPT vision message limits.</p></li></ul><p>Use the controls at the bottom of the screen, like the camera button below, to get started. We are temporarily disabling Browse while we fix this.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>June 22, 2023</h1><h2>Browsing and search on mobile</h2><p>We’ve made two updates to the mobile ChatGPT app:</p><ul><li><p>Browsing: Plus users can now use Browsing to get comprehensive answers and current insights on events and information that extend beyond the model&#x27;s original training data. To try it out, enable Browsing in the “new features” section of your app settings. Then select GPT-4 in the model switcher and choose “Browse with Bing” in the drop-down.</p></li><li><p>Search History Improvements: Tapping on a search result takes you directly to the respective point in the conversation.</p></li></ul></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>May 24, 2023</h1><h2>iOS app available in more countries, shared links in alpha, Bing Plugin, disable history on iOS</h2><h4>ChatGPT app for iOS in more countries</h4><p>Good news! We’re expanding availability of the ChatGPT app for iOS to more countries and regions. Users in 11 countries can now download the ChatGPT app in the Apple App Store including the United States: Albania, Croatia, France, Germany, Ireland, Jamaica, Korea, New Zealand, Nicaragua, Nigeria, and the United Kingdom.<br/><br/>We will continue to roll out to more countries and regions in the coming weeks. You can track the iOS app rollout here.</p><h4>Shared Links</h4><p>We&#x27;re excited to introduce a new feature: shared links. This feature allows you to create and share your ChatGPT conversations with others. Recipients of your shared link can either view the conversation or copy it to their own chats to continue the thread. This feature is currently rolling out to a small set of testers in alpha, with plans to expand to all users (including free) in the upcoming weeks.<br/><br/>To share your conversations:</p><ol><li><p>Click on the thread you’d like to share</p></li><li><p>Select the “Share” button</p></li><li><p>Click on “Copy Link”</p></li></ol><p>Learn more.</p><h4>Bing Plugin</h4><p>Browse with Bing. We’ve integrated the browsing feature - currently in beta for paid users - more deeply with Bing. You can now click into queries that the model is performing. We look forward to expanding the integration soon.</p><h4>Disable chat history on iOS</h4><p>You can now disable your chat history on iOS. Conversations started on your device when chat history is disabled won’t be used to improve our models, won’t appear in your history on your other devices, and will only be stored for 30 days. Similar to the functionality on the web, this setting does not sync across browsers or devices. Learn more.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>May 12, 2023</h1><h2>Web browsing and Plugins are now rolling out in beta</h2><p>If you are a ChatGPT Plus user, enjoy early access to experimental new features, which may change during development. We’ll be making these features accessible via a new beta panel in your settings, which is rolling out to all Plus users over the course of the next week.</p><div class="intercom-container intercom-align-center"><img src="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-412da42b72c5b70efb883b5e/be57ea9e9471945856dd3e210ed2c80b/BetaPanel.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344" width="1468" height="678" srcSet="https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-412da42b72c5b70efb883b5e/be57ea9e9471945856dd3e210ed2c80b/BetaPanel.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=320 320w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-412da42b72c5b70efb883b5e/be57ea9e9471945856dd3e210ed2c80b/BetaPanel.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=480 480w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-412da42b72c5b70efb883b5e/be57ea9e9471945856dd3e210ed2c80b/BetaPanel.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=672 672w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-412da42b72c5b70efb883b5e/be57ea9e9471945856dd3e210ed2c80b/BetaPanel.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=960 960w, https://images.ctfassets.net/j22is2dtoxu1/intercom-img-412da42b72c5b70efb883b5e/be57ea9e9471945856dd3e210ed2c80b/BetaPanel.png?q=80&amp;fm=webp&amp;w=1344 1344w" sizes="(min-width: 792px) 672px, (min-width: 640px) calc(100vw - Each continuation counts towards the message allowance.</p></div><div class="intercom-interblocks-callout callout-transparent"><h1>May 3, 2023</h1><h2>Updates to ChatGPT</h2><p>We’ve made several updates to ChatGPT! Here&#x27;s what&#x27;s new:</p><ul><li><p>You can now turn off chat history and export your data from the ChatGPT settings. Conversations that are started when chat history is disabled won’t be used to train and improve our models, and won’t appear in the history sidebar.</p></li><li><p>We are deprecating the Legacy (GPT-3.5) model on May 10th. You can learn more about plugins here.<br/><br/>Today, we will begin extending plugin access to users and developers from our waitlist. 2025\",[3601,3602,3603],{\"_276\":314,\"_315\":321,\"_317\":-7,\"_280\":3621},{\"_276\":314,\"_315\":316,\"_317\":-7,\"_280\":3618},{\"_276\":287,\"_280\":3604},[3605,3606,3607,3608,3609],{\"_276\":290,\"_290\":3617,\"_292\":-7},{\"_276\":290,\"_290\":3615,\"_292\":3616},{\"_276\":290,\"_290\":3614,\"_292\":-7},{\"_276\":407,\"_26\":3610,\"_280\":3611},{\"_276\":290,\"_290\":1941,\"_292\":-7},\"/en/articles/12293630-chatgpt-pulse\",[3612],{\"_276\":290,\"_290\":3613,\"_292\":-7},\"ChatGPT Pulse\",\". This is a new experience where ChatGPT can now proactively deliver personalized, focused updates for you once a day. These updates are based on asynchronous research ChatGPT can do on your behalf overnight, synthesizing information from your memory, chat history, and direct feedback to learn what’s most relevant to you.\\n\\nUpdates are delivered once a day as visual summaries you can scan at a glance or dive deeper into. Each topic opens into more detail where you can ask follow-up questions. You can shape what shows up in pulse with a quick thumbs up or down, or by using curate to tell ChatGPT what you’d like to see.\\n\\nIf you ask for something new in the evening, we aim to include it the next morning, though exact timing may vary. Pulse refreshes daily, and you can save any item into a chat to keep it.\\n\\nAs a preview, pulse has some limitations. It aims to show you what’s most relevant and useful but you may still see suggestions that miss the mark. For example, you may get tips for a project you already completed. You can guide what shows up by telling ChatGPT directly. It remembers your feedback for next time and improves as it learns from real use.\\n\\nIt requires saved memory and chat history to be turned on, and you can switch pulse off anytime in settings.
